playsound mob.blood @a[r=300] ~~~ 999999 1.75 999999
playsound mob.supernova @a[r=300] ~~~ 999999 1 999999
function damages/blood_damage
scoreboard objectives add projectile_ID dummy
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run function randomstun3
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run damage @s 25 override
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run scoreboard players set @s Stun 50
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run scoreboard players add @s Evade 7
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run effect @s nausea 7 1
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run effect @s poison 7 2
execute as @e[r=3,family=!projectile,family=!damage,family=!barrier,tag=!Frames] at @s unless score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run effect @s slowness 7 1
tag @s add exploding
scoreboard players set found_other projectile_ID 0
execute as @e[type=ds:supernova,tag=!exploding] if score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run scoreboard players set found_other projectile_ID 1
execute if score found_other projectile_ID matches 0 as @e[type=!ds:supernova] if score @s projectile_ID = @e[type=ds:supernova,c=1] projectile_ID run scoreboard players set @s projectile_ID 0
event entity @s ds:disappear
